  scribal_relationship:
    relationship: scribe of
    scribe_certainty: High
    production_info: Colophon
    function_of_copying: Main Text
    scribe_role: Main copyist
    comments: 'Gertrud Tetzel, Nuremberg, Colophon, High, Nuremberg, Stadtbibliothek,
      Cent. VII 11a, Parchment: 1, 180, 189-190, 198, 207-208, 219-220. book cursive
      script by two different hands: 1. 2r-24r and 180r-262r by the hand of Gertraud
      Tetzlin; 2. 25r-179r by the hand of Klara Keiperin On 1r, an old table of contents,
      but the texts listed there are now in a different order in the manuscript, and
      some are missing; the manuscript was later rebound: • “Item in the book, first,
      many beautiful sermons on how one should adorn and prepare the heart for God”
      (now 180r-251r) • “Item, how one should prepare oneself for the Holy Sacrament”
      (154r-179v) • “Item, The Twelve Deaths” (25r-153v) • “Item, Saint Bernardino’s
      sermon on the Passion of Christ” • “Item, On the Ten Commandments” (now missing)
      • “Item, finally, about a holy virgin named Lydeweyg” (252r-262r).'
